IN NICOSIA A PRIEST FROM PHRENAROS, PHODIOS KALOGIROU, WAS 
DETAINED FOR QUESTIONING AFTER POLICE SEARCHED HIS QUARTERS. 
KALOGIROU WAS A COLLEAGUE OF PAPASTAVROS PAPAGATHANGELOU, WHO WAS 
DEPORTED WITH ARCHBISHOP MAKARIOS, LEADER OF THE MOVEMENT FOR UNION 

® 

AIDED BY BLOODHOUNDS, TEAMS SEARCHED EVERY HOUSE IN PHRENAROS. 
AUTHORITIES REPORTED THEY FOUND SIX SHOTGUNS, TWO BOMBS, THREE MAND- 
GRENADES, A HIGH EXPLOSIVE SHELL AND SHOTGUN SHELLS. 

PHRENAROS’ ENTIRE MALE POPULATION WAS ORDERED OUTSIDE FOR 
QUESTIONING. EACH WAS GIVEN PAPER AND TOLD TO WRITE DOWN IN PRIVATE 
WHAT HE KNEW ABOUT THE AMBUSH. FINGERPRINTS OF ALL VILLAGERS ALSO 
WERE TAKEN AND A CURFEW WAS IMPOSED. 

*UNTIL THEY PAY EVERY CENT OF THE FINE, WE SHALL KEEP THIS 
CURFEW ON--NO MATTER WHAT HAPPENS,® A BRITISH OFFICER SAID. 

BRITISH TROOPS CONTINUED THEIR HUNT FOR THE ARSENALS OF EOKA, 

THE REBELS’ FIGHTING UNDERGROUND, IN OTHER PARTS OF THE ISLAND. 
THE GOVERNMENT ANNOUNCED 21 MEN WERE ARRESTED AT YPSONAS VILLAGE 
IN THE LIMASSOL AREA, AFTER LIVE AMMUNITION, TWO MASKS AND OTHER 
SUSPICIOUS ARTICLES WERE FOUND IN THEIR HOMES.

REPORTS OF DISORDERS IN GEORGIA HAVE CHIEFLY INVOLVED TIFLIS 
UNIVERSITY. THE TIFLIS NEWSPAPER DAWN OF THE EAST SAID IN ITS MARCH 
24 EDITION THE UNIVERSITY'S COMMUNIST PARTY SECRETARY HAD BEEN FIRED 
FOR FAILURE TO INDOCTRINATE THE STUDENTS WITH COMMUNISM. IT SAID 
STUDENTS HAD BEEN CUTTING CLASSES WHOLESALE FOR MORE THAN A YEAR 
AND SOME HAVE BEEN GUILTY OF “HOOLIGANISM™ (ANTI-SOCIAL ACTIVITY). 

REPORTS SAID A STUDENT PARADE MARCH 9 FEATURED BANNERS AND PICTURES 
OF STALIN AND LENIN. THE DIRECTOR OF THE UNIVERSITY SAID IN A 
TELEPHONE INTERVIEW, HOWEVER, THAT CLASSES WERE CONTINUING NORMALLY 
AT THE SCHOOL. 

DAWN OF THE EAST INDICATED STUDENTS IN GEORGIA ALREADY WERE SHOWING 
RENEWED SIGNS OF THEIR INTENSE PRIDE IN THEIR SEPARATE NATIONALITY 
LONG BEFORE THE CURRENT KREMLIN CAMPAIGN AGAINST STALIN RULE. 

TODAY'S 7,000-WORD ARTICLE IN PRAVDA WAS SPREAD OVER 12 COLUMNS AND 
BROUGHT HOME TO THE GENERAL RUSSIAN PUBLIC FOR THE FIRST TIME THE

ICELAND IS ABOUT MIDWAY BETWEEN MOSCOW AND NEW YORK. IT IS A 
REFUELING STATION FOR AIRCRAFT FLYING BETWEEN EUROPE AND NORTH AMERICA. 
IT Is ALSO A VITAL STOP FOR U.S. MILITARY AIR TRANSPORT ON THE NORTH

CONSTRUCTING AND MAINTAINING THE DEFENSES OF ICELAND.

KEFLAVIK AND THREE RADAR STATIONS.

CHIEF U.S. DELEGATE HENRY CABOT LODCE JR. PROMISED TO ANSWER THEIR 
QUESTIONS AT THE COUNCIL'S NEXT MEETING ON TUESDAY. 

AT THE OUTSET, LODGE SOUGHT TO REASSURE THE ARABS BY DECLARING 
THE U.S. RESOLUTION HAD NO CONCEALED AIMS. IT HAD NO OTHER PURPOSE, 
HE SAID, EXCEPT TO PREVENT WAR. 

HE Wks FOLLOWED BY THE REPRESENTATIVES OF CUBA. BELGIUM AND 
NATIONALIST CHINA WHO SUPPORTED THE U.S. RESOLUTION IN PRINCIPLE. 
ALL RESERVED THEIR FINAL POSITIONS UNTIL THEY HAD HEARD THE VIEWS 
OF THE INTERESTED PARTIES. BRITAIN, FRANCE AND AUSTRALIA PREVIOUSLY 
HAD PLEDGED THEIR SUPPORT. RUSSIA REMAINED SILENT. 

ISRAEL RESERVED THE RIGHT TO STATE ITS POSITION AT A LATER MEETING.

ANOTHER -E . SF THE BAGHDAD AND SEATO PACTS TODAY 
CHARGING THEY "PUSHED THE WORLD IN THE WRONG DIRECTION" AND “BLOCKED 
RICHT TENDENCIES" WORKING FOR PEACE. 

IN ONE OF HIS SHARPEST ATTACKS ON WESTERN MILITARY ALLIANCES 
NEHRU TOLD PARLIAMENT THEY REPRESENT "A WRONG, DANGEROUS AND HARMFUL 
APPROACH® TO INTERNATIONAL PROBLEMS. 

NEHRU OFTEN HAS LASHED THE WEST’'S MILITARY PACTS, SAYING HE 
BELIEVED THEY DID. NO GOOD. TODAY HE CHARGED THEY "CHECKED FACTORS IN 
THE WORLD TODAY LEADING TOWARD DISARMAMENT AND PEACE.® 

THE BAGHDAD AND SEATO ALLIANCES, HE SAID, AFFECT INDIA DIRECTLY BY 
TENDING TO ENCIRCLE US IN TWO OR THREE DIRECTIONS." BUT HE INDICATED 
HE WAS INDICTING THEM FOR "LARGER REASONS®--FOR WHAT THEY MEANT TO THE 
WORLD-- AS WELL AS FOR THE ®NARROWER REASONS® OF INTEREST CHIEFLY TO 


INDIA. 

THAILAND ON INDIA’S EAST AND PAKISTAN ON THE WEST ARE MEMBERS OF 
SEATO. PAKISTAN IS A MEMBER OF THE BAGHDAD GROUP. 

NEHRU MADE NO SPECIFIC MENTION OF ANY OTHER MILITARY ALLIANCE OR

-THE SENATE ADOPTED A RESOLUTION TODAY 
A HAT THE JUSTIC RTMENT BRING CONTEMPT OF CONGRESS PROCEEDINGS 
AGAINST HARVEY MATUSOW, SELF-DESCRIBED FORMER COMMUNIST AND LIAR, 

THE ACTION WAS TAKEN BY VOICE VOTE AND WITHOUT OBJECTION. 

THE RESOLUTION, PENDING ON THE SENATE CALENDAR SINCE LAST JULY 12, 

WAS SUBMITTED BY THE SENATE INTERNAL SECURITY SUBCOMMITTEE AFTER 
MATUSOW REFUSED TO ANSWER SOME QUESTIONS ASKED HIM AT PUBLIC HEARINGS 
MORE THAN A YEAR AGO. 

CONVICTION FOR CONTEMPT CARRIES A PENALTY UP TO A $1,000 FINE OR A 
YEAR'S IMPRISONMENT, OR BOTH. 

MATUSOW WAS CALLED BEFORE THE SUBCOMMITTEE AFTER HE RECANTED SWORN 
TESTIMONY HE HAD GIVEN ABOUT ALLEGED REDS BEFORE CONGRESSIONAL INVESTI- 
GATING COMMITTEES AND IN FEDERAL COURT TRIALS. THE TURNABOUT WITNESS 
SAID HE HAD FALSELY BRANDED HUNDREDS OF PERSONS AS COMMUNISTS OR 
COMMUNIST SYMPATHIZERS. 

THE SUBCOMMITTEE CHARGED IN A REPORT LAST DECEMBER THAT MATUSOW LIED 
WHEN HE RECANTED HIS EARLIER TESTIMONY AND THAT H E HAD BEEN PAID TO DO 
SO BY A COMMUNIST *BRAIN TRUST. 

WHILE MATUSOW TESTIFIED VOLUBLY AT THE SUBCOMMITTEE HEARINGS LAST 
YEAR, HE REFUSED TO ANSWER A FEW QUESTIONS AND INVOKED HIS FIFTH 
AMENDMENT PROTECTION AGAINST SELF INCRIMINATION, 

ONE THING HE REFUSED TO TELL THE SUBCOMMITTEE WAS THE NAME OF THE 
MANUFACTURER OF A *STRINGLESS YO-YO" HE SAID HE HAD INVENTED.

VIENNA, AUSTRIA, MARCH 30 (AP)-WHILE THE DENUNCIATION OF THE 
STALIN MYTH GOES ON, MANY PARENTS IN POLAND AND OTHER COMMUNIST-RULED 
COUNTRIES ARE GROANING: "HOW CAN WE EXPLAIN THIS TO OUR CHILDREN?" 

THE PLIGHT OF PARENTS AND TEACHERS WHO FOR YEARS TAUGHT CHILDREN 
THAT STALIN WAS GREAT AND FATHERLY GENIUS WAS SUMMED UP BY 
ZYCIE WARSZAWY, OFFICIAL ORGAN OF THE POLISH COMMUNIST GOVERNMENT. 

"OUR READERS KEEP WRITING US: ‘HOW SHALL WE EXPLAIN IT?'™ 

POLAND LEADS ALL THE OTHER SOVIET SATELLITE COUNTRIES IN CONDEMNING 
STALIN» 

ONE OF THE SHARPEST ATTACKS THERE WAS DELIVERED BY JERZY MORAWSKI, 
PROPAGANDA CHIEF OF THE COMMUNIST PARTY'S CENTRAL COMMITTEE. 

WRITING IN THE OFFICIAL PARTY PAPER TRYBUNA LUDU, HE DESCRIBED 
STALIN AS A “BLOODTHIRSTY TYRANT WITH DELUSIONS OF GRANDEUR. A MAN 
WHO MADE MANY HONEST COMMUNISTS THE VICTIMS OF HIS REPRISALS.* 

THE WARSAW PRESS® SAID PARTY WORKERS WERE FIGHTING HARD AGAINST 
DISSILUSIONMENT WITH COMMUNISM WHICH IS SPREADING THROUCH 
POLISH YOUTH ORGANIZATIONS CAUSED BY THE SHOCK OF THE STALIN 
DEBUNKINGs PARTY AGITATORS ARE PREACHING THAT NOT COMMLINISM BUT 
STALIN WAS AT FAULT.

BELGRADE, YUGOSLAVIA, MARCH 30 (AP)-THE NEWSPAPER POLITIKA SAID 
TODAY “IT WOULD BE NORMAL TO EXPECT NEW REHABILITATIONS® IN THE 
FONMUNIST WORLD SIMILAR TO THAT OF THE EXECUTED LASZLO RAJK IN 

THE PAPER CARRIED A PHOTO OF RAJK, HUNGARIAN FOREIGN AND INTERIOR 
MINISTER HANGED AS A TRAITOR AND A CONVICTED *TITOIST™ IN 1949. THE 
HUNGARIAN COMMUNIST PARTY THIS WEEK PROCLAIMED THAT RAJK HAD BEEN 
CONVICTED ON TRUMPED-UP EVIDENCE. 

WHEN STALIN BROKE WITH PRESIDENT TITO OF YUGOSLAVIA IN 1948 
TITOISM BECAME A COMMON CRIME IN THE IRON CURTAIN COUNTRY. THE 
SOVIET COMMUNIST PARTY'S DETHRONEMENT OF STALIN COINCIDES WITH 
MOSCOW'S ATTEMPT TO HEAL THE BREACH WITH YUGOSLAVIA. 


A87 


"THE CONGRESS OF THE RUSSIAN COMMUNIST PARTY," SAID POLITIKA, 
*RIGHTLY JUDGED THAT WITHOUT-THE LIQUIDATION OF STALIN THERE IS 
NO PROGRESS; SO THE LEADERS IN BUDAPEST HAD TO SEE THAT THE 
REHABILITATION OF THE INNOCENT EXECUTED MUST BE A CONSEQUENCE OF THE 
REMOVING OF STALIN FROM HIS FALSE THRONE.” 

A YUGOSLAV GOVERNMENT SPOKESMAN TOLD A NEWS CONFERENCE RAJK'S 
TRIAL "WAS A FRAME-UP BASED ON FORGERIES AND DIRECTED AGAINST 
YUGOSLAVIA.® : 

ASKED IF HUNGARY'S ACTION WOULD INFLUENCE YUGOSLAVIA'S RELATIONS 
WITH BUDAPEST, THE SPOKESMAN REPLIED THAT “DEVELOPMENT OF RELATIONS 
AMONG COUNTRIES IS A QUESTION WHICH HINGES ON MANY THINGS. 

YUGOSLAV HAS STRESSED REPEATEDLY THAT WORDS ARE NOT SUFFICIENT 
TO REMOVE THE DISTRUST CREATED BY THE BREAK WITH THE OTHER COMMUNIST 
COUNTRIES AFTER 1948. THE HUNGARIAN DEVELOPMENT IS SEEN HERE AS THE 
SORT OF ACTION DESIRED BY YUGOSLAVIA. 

THE DEVELOPMENTS IN BULGARIA AND ALBANIA WILL BE WATCHED HERE WITH 
INTEREST. 

THE SAME LEADERS WHO EXECUTED TRAJCO KOSTOV, BULGARIAN DEPUTY 
PREMIER, AND KOCI XOXE, ALBANIAN MINISTER OF INTERIOR, ARE STILL 
RULING THESE TWO COUNTRIES.» THIS IS SEEN HERE AS A BARRIER TO THE 
REHABILITATION OF THE TWO EXECUTED LEADERS, WHO WERE CONDEMNED FOR 
*“TITOISM.*

AMS BUDGET (420) 
PRECEDE RABAT DAY 

ALGIERS, ALGERIA, MARCH 30 (AP)-FRENCH FORCES PRESSED THEIR 
INTENSE ANTI-GUERILLA OPERATIONS THROUGHOUT ALGERIA TODAY. BUT 
THERE WAS A PROMISE OF RELIEF FROM ATTACKS BY REBELLIOUS TRIBESMEN 
IN NEIGHBORING MOROCCO. 

FRENCH SOURCES SAID 66 ALGERIAN NATIONALIST REBELS HAD BEEN KILLED 
WITHIN 24 HOURS IN A WIDESPREAD CAMPAIGN. 

THE BIGGEST CLASH OCCURRED NEAR AKBOU, IN THE MOUNTAINOUS 
CONSTANTINE AREA. EIGHTEEN REBELS WERE KILLED THERE AND ANOTHER WAS 
WOUNDED BY HELICOPTER-BORNE TROOPS. A LARGE QUANTITY OF AMMUNITION 
WAS SEIZED, IN ADDITION TO 20 RIFLES AND OTHER GUERRILLA EQUIPMENT. 
THE FRENCH FORCES REPORTED NO LOSSES ON THEIR SIDE. 

SEVERAL SMALLER BANDS OF REBELS WERE AMBUSHED IN AN AREA EXTENDING 
50 MILES AROUND CONSTANTINE. THEY LEFT A TOTAL OF 28 DEAD ON THE 
GROUND.» A FRENCH SOLDIER WAS KILLED IN ONE OF THE CLASHES, NEAR BIR 

IN THE ORAN AREA, A VIOLENT CLASH RESULTED IN THE DEATH OF FOUR 
FRENCH SOLDIERS AND INJURIES TO FIVE OTHERS. THE STRONG REBEL BAND 
FLED, LEAVING SIX DEAD BEHIND. 

IN THE GREAT KABYLIE MOUNTAINS, 10 REBELS WERE KILLED, AND NINE 
OTHERS, INCLUDING TWO WOUNDED, WERE CAPTURED. ROADS WERE CUT IN 
THE SAME AREA AND A VILLAGE SCHOOL SET AFIRE. TWO PRO-FRENCH 
ALGERIANS WERE ASSASSINATED, AND FOUR FRENCH SOLDIERS WERE WOUNDED. 

IN MOROCCO, 30 YOUNG CHIEFTAINS OF THE OUTLAW “LIBERATION ARMY" 
KNELT BEFORE SULTAN MOHAMMED BEN YOUSSEF AND PROMISED TO LAY DOWN 
THEIR ARMS. THE MONARCH CALLED THEM *MY FAITHFUL AND LOYAL SUBJECT.*® 

THE BEARDED MEN, AGED BETWEEN 25 AND 30, CAME TO THE ROYAL 
CAPITAL, RABAT, FROM THE FEZ AREA WHERE FIGHTING RAGED LONG AFTER 
THE SULTAN RECOVERED HIS THRONE AND OTHER MOROCCAN NATIONALISTS HAD 
CEASED ATTACKING THE FRENCH. 

THE SULTAN THANKED THE CHIEFTAINS *FOR THE EFFORTS YOU NEVER 
STOPPED MAKING FOR THE BENEFIT OF THE MOTHERLAND AND THE THRONE. ®

NICOSIA, CYPRUS, MARCH 30 (AP) -THE BRITISH ANNOUNCED HERE 
TONIGHT THEY ARE JAMMING A GREEK PROPAGANDA BROADCAST FROM A STATION 
IN GREECE THAT ALSO CARRIES VOICE OF AMERICA PROGRAMS. 

UeSe CONSUL RAYMOND COURTNEY SAID HE UNDERSTOOD THE STATION, IN 
SALONIKA, IS JOINTLY OWNED BY THE UNITED STATES AND GREECE. 

A BRITISH GOVERNMENT SPOKESMAN SAID THE VOICE BROADCASTS 
THEMSELVES ARE NOT BEING JAMMED. BUT A GREEK PROGRAM CALLED 
VOICE OF THE FATHERLAND IS BEING BLOCKED A HALF HOUR BEFORE THE 
REGULAR AMERICAN BROADCAST AT 9 PeMey CYPRUS TIME. 

THE SO-CALLED FATHERLAND PROGRAMS HAVE BEAMED A STREAM OF ANTI- 
BRITISH PROPAGANDA AT CYPRUS. 

BRITAIN ALREADY HAD JAMMED THE BROADCASTS OVER OTHER STATIONS 
ON GROUNDS THAT THEY WERE INCITING CYPRIOTS TO VIOLENCE. 

LAST WEEK FATHERLAND STARTED TRANSMITTING OVER THE JOINTLY 
OPERATED SALONIKA STATION WITH WHAT IT CALLED A GREEK NATIONAL 
PROGRAMe JAMMING OF THIS BEGAN TODAY FOR THE. FIRST TIME, A 
BRITISH SPOKESMAN SAID. 

“IT IS THE POLICY OF THE GOVERNMENT TO JAM ANY PROVOCATIVE 


BROADCASTS TO CYPRUS FROM WHATEVER SOURCE IT MAY EMANATE," HE DECLARED. 


THE SALONIKA STATION IS ONE OF THE MOST POWERFUL IN THIS PART OF 
THE MEDITERRANEAN. NORMALLY THE UNITED STATES GOVERNMENT 
BUILDS THE VOICE STATIONS AND THEN MAKES ARRANGEMENTS WITH LOCAL 
AUTHORITIES FOR SHARING AIR TIME OVER THEM, 

THE JAMMING OF GREEK BROADCASTS TO CYPRUS HAS BEEN A HOT 
ISSUE HERE AND IN GREAT BRITAIN. IT HAS BEEN SHARPLY DEBATED 
IN PARLIAMENT.

NIGHT LEAD JETS 
PARIS, MARCH 30 (AP)-FRANCE HAS BEGUN DELIVERYOF 12 MYSTERE 
JET INTERCEPTOR PLANES TO ISRAEL WITH TACIT UeSe CONSENT, RELIABLE 
SOURCES SAID TODAY. THERE WAS NO OFFICIAL CONFIRMATION OR DENIAL. 
THE INFORMED, UNOFFICIAL SOURCES REPORTED SOME OF THE JETS ALREADY 
HAD PASSED THROUGH ROME. 
FRANCE, BRITAIN AND THE UNITED STATES HAVE BEEN HOPING TO HEAD OFF 
AN ARMAMENT RACE IN THE EXPLOSIVE MIDDLE EAST BY TRYING TO CUT OFF ALL 
ARMS SHIPMENTS. 
EGYPT HAS GOT IRON CURTAIN ARMS FROM CZECHOSLOVAKIA AND RUSSIA. 
SINCE LAST SUMMER AND UPSET A BALANCE THE WEST HAD HOPED TO MAINTAIN. 
FRANCE CONTRACTED TO SUPPLY THE MYSTERES, THE STANDARD FRENCH 
AIR FORCE FIGHTER PLANE, BEFORE THE WESTERN AGREEMENT. 
DELIVERIES HAD BEEN DELAYED WHILE FRANCE SOUGHT TO WORK OUT A COMMON 


POLICY WITH BRITAIN AND THE UNITED STATES ON SUCH SHIPMENTS IN THE 
LIGHT OF THE RED ARMS SHIPMENTS TO EGYPT. 

FRENCH FOREIGN MINISTER CHRISTIAN PINEAU ONCE INDICATED THAT IF THE 
THREE POWERS COULD AGREE ON AN IRONCLAD EMBARGO TO THE MIDDLE EAST, 
FRANCE MIGHT HOLD OFF DELIVERY OF THE JETS. 

HE Pete utes hones IF THERE WAS NO ALLIED BACKING ON THE SUB- 


JECT, FRANCE WOULD HONOR THE CONTRACT ON ITS OWN RESPONSIBILITY. 

A° SERIES OF THREE-POWER MEETINGS IN WASHINGTON FAILED TO PRODUCE 
ANY CLEAR-CUT STAND. 

THE OUTPUT OF MYSTERES IS BOUGHT BY THE UNITED STATES AND TURNED 
OVER TO EUROPEAN NATO COUNTRIES UNDER THE U.Se MILITARY AID 
PROGRAM. 

IT WAS LEARNED THAT AMERICAN AUTHORITIES HAD AGREED TO LET THE 
FRENCH TAKE THE ISRAELI SHIPMENT FROM ITS CURRENT PRODUCTION IN ORDER 
TO FULFILL THE CONTRACT. 

ISRAEL BOUGHT 10 MYSTERES LAST YEAR AND HAS SOME BRITISH FIGHTERS. 

EXPERTS ESTIMATE THE ENTIRE ISRAELI INTERCEPTOR FORCE AT FEWER THAN 
40 PLANES. 

THE EGYPTIANS ARE REPORTED TO BE RECEIVING 200 RUSSIAN MIGI5 
FIGHTERS AND 60 RUSSIAN ILYUSHIN TWIN-JET BOMBERS. 

THE UNITED STATES STILL HAS PENDING AN ISRAELI REQUEST FOR 65 
MILLION DOLLARS WORTH OF ARMS.

TOKYO, MARCH 30 CAP)-JAPAN WILL REMAIN STRICTLY NEUTRAL IN THE 
MIDDLE EAST ARMS RACE, A FOREIGN OFFICE SPOKESMAN SAID TODAY. BUT 
HE ADDED THAT WHETHER JAPAN SHOULD EXPORT ARMS TO ISRAEL OR THE 
ARAB NATIONS IS BEING STUDIED CAREFULLY. 

MITSUO TANAKA, FOREIGN OFFICE PUBLIC INFORMATION CHIEF, DENIED A 
JAPANESE PRESS REPORT THAT SYRIA IS TRYING AT THIS TIME TO BUY

E ISENHOWER-CANADIANS 

WASHINGTON, MARCH 30 (AP)-PRESIDENT EISENHOWER IS REPORTED TO HAVE 
TOLD CANADIAN’ PRIME MINISTER LOUIS STsLAURENT EMPHATICALLY THAT U.S. 
RECOGNITION OF COMMUNIST CHINA IS OUT OF THE QUESTION. 

INFORMANTS SAID EISENHOWER ALSO RULED OUT U.S.APPROVAL OF RED 
CHINESE MEMBERSHIP IN THE UNITED NATIONS, WHEN THE SUBJECT CAME UP 
IN CONVERSATIONS AT WHITE SULPHUR SPRINGS. 

FISENHOWER DECLARED THAT PUBLIC OPINION IN THE UNITED STATES WOULD 
NOT TOLERATE EITHER CONCESSION TO THE CHINESE REDS. PERSONS FAMILIAR 
WITH THE BRIEF DISCUSSIONS HELD ON THIS SUBJECT DURING THE CONFERENCE 
OF NORTH AMERICAN HEADS OF GOVERNMENT SAID THAT THEY WERE CARRIED ON 
IN A FRIENDLY TONE BUT THERE WAS NO MISTAKING THE EMPHATIC NATURE OF 
EISENHOWER'S STATEMENT. 

LESTER B. PEARSON, CANADIAN MINISTER OF EXTERNAL AFFAIRS 
WHO ALSO WAS PRESENT AT THE TALK, TOLD THE PRESIDENT AND SECRETARY OF 
STATE DULLES THAT CANADIAN PUBLIC OPINION WAS BECOMING IMPATIENT 
WITH THE PRESENT STATE OF AFFAIRS, BUT REPORTEDLY MADE IT CLEAR THAT 
*ANADA DOES NOT PLAN ANY SUDDEN CHANGE OF ITS OWN POLICIES IN 
RESPECT TO THE CHINESE SITUATION. 

CANADA, UNLIKE BRITAIN, HAS NOT RECOGNIZED RED CHINA, IT HAS 
CONTINUED ITS FORMAL RECOGNITION OF THE REGIME OF GENERALISSIMO CHIANG 
KAI-SHEK ON FORMOSA, BUT HAS ALLOWED DIPLOMATIC RELATIONS BETWEEN OTTAWA 
AND TAIPEI TO LAPSE. 

DIPLOMATIC INFORMANTS SAID THE SUBJECT OF RED CHINA WAS FIRST 
BROUGHT UP AT WHITE SULPHUR SPRINGS IN THE COURSE OF A GENERAL 
DISCUSSION OF FOREIGN AFFAIRS AMONG EISENHOWER, STsLAURENT AND MEXICAN 
PRESIDENT ADOLFO RUIZ GORTINES AND THEIR ADVISERS ON TUESDAY MORNING, 

AT THAT TIME DULLES GAVE A GENERAL REVIEW OF HIS RECENT TOUR 
THROUGH SOUTH ASIA AND THE FAR EASTe PEARSON IS REPORTED TO HAVE SAID 
THAT THE FORMOSA-RED CHINA PROBLEM HAD NOT BEEN MENTIONED BY DULLES 
AND TO HAVE INQUIRED ABOUT THE AMERICAN VIEW ON IT. THIS LED INTO A 
BRIEF DISCUSSION OF THE NATIONALIST-HELD COASTAL ISLANDS OF QUEMOY 
AND THE MATSUS. DULLES IS REPORTED TO HAVE SAID THAT IT WOULD BE IM- 
POSSIBLE TO SEPARATE THOSE ISLANDS FROM FORMOSA BECAUSE CHIANG KAI- 

SHEK REGARDS THEM AS INTEGRAL PARTS OF HIS TERRITORY. 

THE COASTAL ISLANDS ARE INVOLVED IN THE TERRITORIAL DISPUTE BETWEEN 
NATIONALIST AND COMMUNIST CHINA AND THERE HAS LONG BEEN SPECULATION THAT 
IF THE REDS WERE GIVEN QUEMOY AND THE MATSUS, THEY MIGHT AGREE TO AN 
ACCEPTABLE FORMOSA SETTLEMENT. DULLES AND ELSENHOWER HAVE NEVER SHARED 
THIS VIEW, AND APPARENTLY MADE THAT CLEAR AT THE WHITE SULPHUR 
SPRINGS CONFERENCE. 


TUESDAY AFTERNOON U.S.sLEADERS MET SEPARATELY WITH THE CANADIAN AND 
MEXICAN OFFICIALS. 


IT WAS IN THIS TWO-WAY TALK WITH THE CANADIANS THAT THE SUBJECT 
OF RECOGNITION AND UNITED NATIONS MEMBERSHIP AROSE. 


‘ 4 
&gt; 








THE FIRM WAY IN WHICH EISENHOWER REJECTED THE WHOLE IDEA OF A CHANGE 
IN UNITED STATES POLICY WAS SAID TO BE ALMOST WORD FOR WORD THE SAME 
THING HE HAD TOLD BRITISH PRIME MINISTER EDEN TWO MONTHS EARLIER, 

INFORMANTS SAID THAT ST.LAURENT AND PEARSON DID NOT PRESS THE 
POINT OF CHANGE, AND THAT THERE WAS NO EFFORT ON EITHER SIDE TO TRY 
TO GET THE OTHER TO MODIFY ITS POLICIES. BRITAIN AND CANADIAN AND 
SEVERAL OTHER COUNTRIES WHICH WOULD BE WILLING TO HAVE COMMUNIST CHINA 
SEATED IN .THE UNITED NATIONS HAVE FOR SEVERAL YEARS DELAYED ANY ACTION 
TO THAT END IN ORDER TO AVOID A BREAK WITH THE UNITED STATES OVER THE 
CHINA ISSUE. THEY ALL PLAN TO CONTINUE TO AVOID ANY DISRUPTIVE ACTION 
THROUGHOUT THIS YEAR OF AMERICAN PRESIDENTIAL CONTEST. AMERICAN 
OFFICIALS ARE BY NO MEANS CERTAIN, HOWEVER, THAT THIS LINE OF 
COOPERATION CAN CONTINUE TO BE HELD WHEN THE NATIONAL POLITICAL DEBATE 


IS OVER.

MARCH 30 (AP)-FOREIGN AID CHIEF JOHN BeHOLLISTER SAID 


TODAY HE HAS 'NO EVIDENCE OF “ANY DIRECT TRADE* BETWEEN NATIONALIST 
AND RED CHINA, THUS ENDING WHAT APPEARED TO HAVE BEEN A DISAGREEMENT 
WITH THE STATE DEPARTMENT. 

NNDERSECRETARY OF STATE HERBERT HOOVER JR» HAD TOLD THE SENATE 
INVESTIGATIONS SUBCOMMITTEE YESTERDAY THAT ANY DEALINGS BETWEEN THE 
CHINESE NATIONALISTS AND THE COMMUNISTS “TOOK PLACE THROUGH HONG 
KONG." THE BRITISH CROWN COLONY ON THE CHINESE MAINLAND.

THE NATIONALISTS AND THE REDS WERE CARRYING ON TRADE WORTH 
MILLIONS OF DOLLARSe 

BUT HOOVER'S LATEST STATEMENT SEEMINGLY CONFLICTED WITH A REPORT

TOKYO, SATURDAY, MAKCH 31 (AP)-THE LAST MAJOR JAPANESE WAR CRIMINAL 


LEFT SUGAMO PRISON TODAY. 


MAJe GEN» KENRYO SATO, 60, WARTIME CHIEF OF THE MILITARY 
AFFAIRS BUREAU OF THE JAPA 


NESE WAR DEPARTMENT, HAD SERVED 10 YEA 
A LIFE SENTENCE. E , EARS OF 


HE WAS THE LAST TO BE PAROLED OF THE JAPANESE WAR LEADERS 
SENTENCED TO PRISON BY THE 11-NATION ALLIED MILITARY TRIBUNAL. 

THE TRIBUNAL HANGED SEVEN, INCLUDING HIDEKI TOJO, WHO TOOK 
OVER AS PRIME MINISTER AND WAR MINISTER FIVE DAYS BEFORE THE PEARL 
HARBOR ATTACK. 

SIX OF THE 18 DIED IN PRISON. ELEVEN OTHERS, INCLUDING PRESENT 
FOREIGN MINISTER MAMORU SHIGEMITSU, WERE PAROLED OVER A PERIOD OF 
YEARS. AFTER SERVING PORTIONS OF THEIR TERMSe PAROLES WERE GRANTED 
eae OF A MAJORITY OF THE NATIONS REPRESENTED ON THE 

B . 

SUGAMO STILL HOLDS SOME 400 CLASS B AND CLASS C WAR CRIMINALS 
JF LOWER RANK. THEY WERE SENTENCED BY MILITARY COURTS OF INDIVIDUAL 
ALLIED NATIONSe MOST OF THEM ARE EXPECTED TO BE PAROLED THIS YEAR. 

SATO, NATTILY DRESSED IN A LIGHT GRAY DOUBLE BREASTED SUIT AND 
~OOKING HEALTHY AND YOUNGER THAN HIS 60 YEARS, HAD NOTHING TO SAY 
{BOUT HIS CONVICTION AND SENTENCE, 

HE SAID HE HAD NO COMPLAINTS ABOUT HIS TREATMENT IN PRISON AND THAT 
iE HAD NO FUTURE PLANS. ‘

SECOND NIGHT LEAD DISARMAMENT 

BY EDWIN SHANKE 

LONDON. MARCH 31 (AP)-RUSSIA UNFOLDED A DISARMAMENT PLAN TODAY TO 
FREEZE ARMS, REDUCE ARMIES, BAN ATOMIC TESTS AT ONCE, AND FORBID THE 
STORING OF ATOMIC WEAPONS IN GERMANY. 

THE PLAN--OUTLINED PUBLICLY BY MOSCOW RADIO AFTER IT WAS DELIVERED 
IN SECRET TO THE DISARMAMENT CONFERENCE HERE WEDNESDAY--WAS GREETED 
WITH RESERVE BY Ue Se EXPERTS IN LONDON. 

THE PLAN APPEARED TO GO A LONG WAY TOWARD PRESIDENT EISENHOWER'S 
®OPEN SKY® INSPECTION TO PREVENT SECRET ARMING AND MASSING OF 
TROOPS. BUT Us. Se 911838-)* 8, 2-°HINGTON SAID A CLOSE

THE RUSSIANS SAID THAT AERIAL PHOTOGRAPHY COULD BE USED AS ONE 
MEANS OF INSPECTION ONLY AFTER ®CONFIDENCE BETWEEN THE STATES 
HAS BEEN CONSOLIDATED."

REFLECTING THE KREMLIN'S NEW CORDIALITY, RUSSIA'S DELEGATE ANDREI 
GROMYKO APPEARS TO HAVE MADE ONLY TWO VAGUE REFERENCES TO NUCLEAR DIS- 
ARMAMENT--A BAN ON NUCLEAR TESTS AND A VIEW PARALLELING THE AMERICAN 
THAT AGREEMENT ON CONVENTIONAL DISARMAMENT COULD ESTABLISH A BETTER AT- 
MOSPHERE FOR TAKING UP NUCLEAR WEAPONS. THE FRENCH QUICKLY EXPRESSED 
THEIR DISAPPOINTMENT WITH THE RUSSIAN PLAN WHICH THEY REGARD AS "A 
BACKWARD STEP® BECAUSE IT TAKES NO DECISIVE STEP IN THE ATOMIC FIELD. 

LITHER THE U.sSe NOR THE RUSSIAN PROPOSALS GO AS FAR AS A SWEEPING 
"9%" “LSH*FRENCH PLAN. THIS CALLS FOR AN IMMEDIATE HALT IN THE ARMS RACE

s\Y HAVE THE RUSSIANS SWITCHED EMPHASIS FROM NUCLEAR TO CONVENTIONAL 

&gt;? WESTERN EXPERTS ARE PUZZLED. 

OME BELIEVE IT COULD MEAN THAT THE SOVIETS NOW FEEL THEY HAVE 

34T UP WITH THE UNITED STATES IN ATOMIC WEAPON OUTPUT AND COULD 
AFFORD TO MAKE CONCESSIONS ON CONVENTIONAL DISARMAMENT AND CONTROL. 
OTHER FACTOR IS THAT NUCLEAR DISARMAMENT COULD HARDLY WORK AS LONG AS 
SCIENTISTS STILL HAVE TO DEVELOP FOOLPROOF METHODS OF CONTROLLING NU- 
CLEAR PRODUCTION OR DETECTING STOCKPILES. 

IN’ SIDESTEPPING NUCLEAR DISARMAMENT AT THIS TIME, THE RUSSIANS ALSO 
APPE.~ TC HAVE APPROACHED A VIEW EXPRESSED BY FORMER BRITISH FOREIGN 
MINISTER HAROLD MACMILLAN AT LAST AUTUMN'S BIG FOUR GENEVA CONFERENCE. 
Hc SUGGESTED THEN THE POWERS SHOULD CONCENTRATE ON DOING SOMETHING TO 
REDUCE CONVENTIONAL ARMS AS LONG AS AGREEMENT ON NUCLEAR DISARMAMENT 
SEEMED A LONG WAY OFF. 

U.sSe THINKING ON DISARMAMENT STRESSES THE QUESTION OF EFFECTIVE 
CONTROL RIGHT DOWN THE LINE--ON MANPOWER, ARMS AND MILITARY SPENDING-- 
TO ELIMINATE SUSPICION AND THE CHANCES of CHEATING. 

THE RUSSIANS FOR THE FIRST TIME HAVE ACCEPTED IN PRINCIPLE 
FISENHOWER'S “OPEN SKIES" ARMS INSPECTION SCHEME. THIS WOULD PERMIT 
THE MAJOR POWERS TO CARRY OUT AERIAL RECONNAISSANCE OVER ONE 
ANOTHER'S TERRITORIES TO ENSURE ALL WERE LIVING UP TO ANY DISARMAMENT

LONDON, MARCH 31 C(AP)-GEORGI MALENKOV 
FAILURE AS SOVIET PREM 
THE BRITISH. 


JUST OVER TWO WEEKS AGO THE PUDGY EX-PREMIER STEPPED FROM A 


A SELF-CONFESSED 
IER, HAS PULLED OFF A HUGE PERSONAL TRIUMPH WITH 


CLOSELY-GUARDED PLANE AT THE LONDON AIRPORT, LOOKING NERVOUS, SELF- 
CONSCIOUS AND NONE TOO SURE OF HIS RECEPTION ON HIS FIRST TRIP OUT- 
white eS CURTAIN, 

’ R A MARATHON ROUND OF PATTING BABIES AND TALKING SMOOTH 
PLEASANTRIES, .MALENKOV IS THE STAR OF A SOVIET PROPAGANDA CIRCUS OF A 
KIN NEVER BEFORE SEEN IN THE WEST. , 


$0.24- 6071 


TO MOST-BRITONS THE FACT OF MALENKOV'S SURVIVAL UNDER THE SOVIET 
REGIME: WAS ASTONISHING ENOUGH. HE WAS, AFTER ALL, THE “YES AND EARS*® 
OF STALIN AND HEIR TO STALIN'S DICTATORSHIP. 

BUT THAT MALENKOV SHOULD TURN UP IN BRITAIN IN HIS NEW JOB OF 
SOVIET MINISTER OF POWER STATIONS, OSTENSIBLY TO INSPECT BRITISH 
POWER PLANTS BUT ACTING LIKE AN OLD TIME ELECTION CANDIDATE, WAS 
SIMPLY AMAZING, 

HIS METHODS ARE SIMPLE. WHENEVER POSSIBLE HE PUSHES ASIDE HIS 
TOUGH SECURITY GUARDS AND GETS AMONG A CROWD OF PEOPLE. WITH AN 
INTERPRETER CLOSE BY, HE CHATS ABOUT KIDS AND HOMES AND HANDS OUT 
“PEACE BADGES*® AND CANDY. 

ALL THIS HE MANAGES WITHOUT LOSS OF DIGNITY AND WITH A FETCHING 
AIR OF MODESTY. IF SOME BABY-PATTING ROUTIE IS MISSED BY THE 
PHOTOGRAPHERS, MALENKOV PATIENTLY GOES THROUGH IT AGAIN TO MAKE 
SURE HIS BIG SMILE GETS IN THE PAPERS--WHICH IT DOES. 

AT NIGHT HE IS THE STAR OF THE TELEVISION NEWSREELS. PEOPLE 
THROUGHOUT BRITAIN SEE HIM REPRESENTED AS A SORT OF GENIAL UNCLE 
MAKING LITTLE JOKES AND PATTING EVERY KID WITHIN RANGE OF HIS CHUBBY 
HANDS. 

ASKED WHAT HE THINKS OF BRITAIN HE TROTS OUT HIS STOCK REPLY3 
“THE FINEST THING WE‘ VESSEEN IN BRITAIN IS THE WONDERFUL BRITISH 
PEOPLE." 


A1z2 
A COLUMNIST IN THE SCOTSMAN, A CONSERVATIVE NEWSPAPER NOT EASILY 
MOVED TO RAPTURES ABOUT COMMUNISTS, CALLED MALENKOV A VIRTUOSO OF 
DIPLOMACY. 
tp SHE HAS MADE IT ABUNDANTLY CLEAR,® SAID THE COLUMNIST, "THAT HE 
LIKES PEOPLE AND DISLIKES STARCH. THAT MAY BE AN ACT ON HIS PART BUT, 
IF IT IS, IT IS A BRILLIANT ACT.* 

THE SPECTATOR, A CONSERVATIVE WEEKLY, SAID? “MR. MALENKOV HAS 
ASTONISHED EVERYONE BY BEING HUMAN." 

WHETHER MALENKOV’ S VISIT HAS HAD ANY REAL EFFECT ON THE POLITICAL 
THINKING OF ORDINARY BRITISH PEOPLE IS DIFFICULT TO JUDGE. 

NOTHING MALENKOV HAS DONE COULD DIMINISH THE CHORUS OF OUTRAGE 
AROUSED BY LAST WEEK'S VISIT HERE OF GEN. IVAN SEROV, RUSSIAN SECURITY 
CHIEF PREPARING FOR THE ARRIVAL APRIL 18 OF SOVIET PREMIER BULGANIN AND 
COMMUNIST CHIEFTAIN KHRUSHCHEV FOR A 10-DAY TOUP OF BRITAIN, 

NOR: ARE PRIME MINISTER EDEN AND HIS GOVERNMENT LIKELY TO BE TAKEN 
IN BY THE RED LEADERS’ SMILES AND PROTESTATIONS OF GOOD FAITH. 

IN TALKS WITH BULGANIN AND KHRUSHCHEV, EDEN UNDOUBTEDLY WILL WANT 
EVIDENCE OF CONCRETE ACTIONS BEFORE BEING CONVINCED OF A CHANGE IN 
ATMOSPHERE IN THE KREMLIN. 

THE SCOTSMAN SUGGESTED EDITORIALLY THAT THE IMPRESSION MADE py 
MALENKOV WOULD BE NEITHER PROFOUND NOR PERMANENT. 

“AFFABLE BEHAVIOR WILL NOT MAKE CONVERTS TO COMMUNIS.", IT satp 
“THE BRITISH PEOPLE ARE NOT EASILY INFLUENCED OR STAMPFDED,* ' 

ON THE OTHER HAND, MALENKOV, GETTING HIS FIRST GLIMPSE OF THE 
WORKINGS OF FREEDOM IN THE WESTERN WORLD, HAS SEEN WITH HIS OWN Eyps 
THAT THE BRITISH PEOPLE ARE NOT DOWN-TRODDEN HIRELINGS OF 
CAPITALISM=-AS PICTURED BY THE SOVIET PRESS--AND THAT THEY ARE Fry 
CLOTHED AND HOUSED TO A STANDARD BEYOND THE DREAMS OF THE AVERAGE ” 

IN INFORMAL CONVERSATIONS WITH THIS CORRESPONDENT AND OTHERS, yp 
EXPRESSED DEEP ADMIRATION FOR THE NEATNESS AND PROSPEROUS AIR OF THF 
BRITISH COUNTRYSIDE AND THE OBVIOUS EFFICIENCY OF BRITISH FARMING,” 
THE THIS IS A SMALL ISLAND,* HE SAID, ®AND IT IS WONDERFUL THE way 

BRITISH HAVE MADE USE OF EVERY INCH OF THEIR LAND.

AP FOREIGN SERVICE ADVANCE FOR AMS SUNDAY, APRIL 1 . 

(ADVANCE) MUNICH, GERMANY, MARCH 31 CAP)-THE DP CAMP, ONCE A 
SYMBOL FOR MUCH OF THE HUMAN SUFFERING OF WORLD WAR II, PASSED OUT 


—- TODAY==-NEARLY 11 YEARS AFTER THE END OF FIGHTING IN 


_DP STANDS FOR “DISPLACED PERSONS® -- SUCH AS THE MILLIONS OF JEWS 
LIBERATED FROM NAZI CONCENTRATION CAMPS AND THE CAPTIVE SLAVE LABORERS 
FORCED TO WORK FOR HITLER'S REICH. 

THE DP CAMPS ORIGINALLY WERE ESTABLISHED BY THE ALLIES TO PROVIDE 
ai VICTIMS OF THE WARe THEY WERE PUT UNDER GERMAN CONTROL IN 


AT ONE TIME POSTWAR GERMANY WAS FULL OF DP CAMPS, BUT GRADUALLY 
THE DISPLACED PERSONS FILTERED OUT UNTIL ONLY ONE CAMP REMAINED, 


FOEHRENWALD NEAR MUNICH. 
TODAY, CAMP FOEHRENWALD, SOLELY FOR JEWS, OFFICIALLY CLOSED SHOP 
AS A DP GAMP. BAVARIAN AUTHORITIES SOLD IT TO THE MUNICH ROMAN 
CATHOLIC DIOCESE AND FROM NOW ON IT WILL BE USED BY CHURCH AUTHORIT- 
IES FOR RESETTLING ETHNIC GERMANS EXPELLED FROM THE EAST. 

IT WILL RESEMBLE SCORES OF OTHER REFUGEE CAMPS SCATTERED ALL OVER 

REFUGEE CAMPS RESULTED FROM THE COLD WAR AND WERE ESTABLISHED 

BY THE GERMANS. THEIR PURPOSE WAS TO TAKE CARE OF THE THOUSANDS OF 
PERSONS WHO FLED WEST TO ESCAPE COMMUNIST OPPRESSION. 

BAVARIA, OF WHICH MUNICH IS THE CAPITAL, OPERATED CAMP FOEHRENWALD. 
BUILT AT FIRST TO HOUSE FAMILIES OF 300 WORKERS IN A WARTIME GERMAN 
AMMUNITION PLANT, IT ONCE WAS THE HOME OF SEVERAL THOUSAND DP'S. 

WHEN THE DECISION CAME TO CLOSE THE CAMP, ONLY 700 PERSONS 
REMAINED. HOMES FOR THESE, MOSTLY AGED PERSONS UNABLE TO WORK, 

WERE FOUND IN HAMBURG, FRANKFURT, STUTTGART, COLOGNE AND OTHER 
WEST GERMAN CITIES.

(ADVANCE) COLOMBO, CEYLON, MARCH 31 (AP)-PRIME MINISTER SIR JOHN 
KOTELAWELA IS STRIVING TO KEEP HIS GOVERNMENT IN POWER IN NEXT WEEK'S 
NATIONAL ELECTIONS ON A PROGRAM OF *ANTI-COMMUNIST NEUTRALISM. 

A SPOKESMAN FOR HIS GOVERNING UNITED NATIONAL PARTY SAID SIR JOHN'S 
FOLLOWERS, WHO HELD A SUBSTANTIAL MAJORITY IN THE LAST CEYLON 
PARLIAMENT, FAVOR NONINVOLVEMENT IN THE WORLD'S COLD WAR BUT 
“IDEOLOGICALLY ARE WITH THE WEST." |

DISSOLVED ON THE ISSUE OF DROPPING ENGLISH IN FAVOR OF SINHALESE | 
| AS CEYLON'S NATIONAL LANGUAGE. THE GOVERNING PARTY AND ITS MAIN 
| OPPOSITION, THE FREEDOM PARTY, SUPPORT THE PROPOSAL; OTHER MINORITIES

"(ON RUBBER AND TEA PLANTATIONS.

WASHINGTON, MARCH 31 (AP) -PRESIDENT EISENHOWER REPORTEDLY HAS RE- 
STATED --IN EMPHATIC TERMS--AMERICAN REFUSAL TO RECOGNIZE COMMUNIST 
CHINA OR TO APPROVE ITS ADMISSION TO THE UNITED NATIONS. . 

INFORMANTS SAID YESTERDAY THE MATTER WAS RAISED BY CANADIAN 
LEADERS DURING THE LITTLE SUMMIT TALKS EARLIER THIS WEEK AT WHITE 
SULPHUR SPRINGS, WeVAe MEXICAN LEADERS ALSO TOOK PART IN THE THREE- 
NATION CONFERENCE, BUT REPORTEDLY WERE NOT INVOLVED IN THE UsSe- 
CANADIAN DISCUSSIONS ON RED CHINA. 

EISENHOWER WAS SAID TO HAVE WORDED HIS REJECTION OF ANY CHANGE 
IN UseSe POLICY TOWARD RED CHINA IN VIRTUALLY THE SAME WAY AS HE HAD 
STATED THIS GOVERNMENT'S VIEWS DURING A MEETING WITH BRITISH PRIME 
MINISTER EDEN TWO MONTHS EARLIER. 

PERSONS IN A POSITION TO KNOW WHAT WENT ON IN THE BRIEF CANADIAN- 
AMERICAN TALK ON RED CHINA SAID EISENHOWER ASSERTED THAT AMERICAN 
PUBLIC OPINION WOULD NOT TOLERATE RECOGNITION OF THE PEIPING 
REGIME, OR U.Se ACCEPTANCE OF RED CHINESE MEMBERSHIP IN THE U.Ne 

CANADIAN PRIME MINISTER LOUIS ST. LAURENT AND LESTER B.PEARSON, 
CANADA'S MINISTER OF EXTERNAL AFFAIRS, WERE SAID NOT TO HAVE 


PRESSED THE POINT IN THE TALKS WHICH REPORTEDLY WERE CARRIED ON IN A 
FRIENDLY TONE. 


HOWEVER, THEY WERE UNDERSTOOD TO HAVE TOLD EISENHOWER AND 
SECRETARY OF STATE DULLES THAT PUBLIC OPINION IN CANADA IS BECOMING 
IMPATIENT WITH THE PRESENT SITUATION, WHICH FINDS CANADA ALSO WITH- 
HOLDING RECOGNITION OF RED CHINA. 

THE CANADIANS REPORTEDLY ASSURED THE AMERICAN LEADERS THAT CANADA 
DOES NOT PLAN ANY SUDDEN REVISION OF ITS POLICIES ON COMMUNIST

PEALS HAS GIVEN THE ARMED SERVICES FRESH BACKING IN THEIR CLAIMED TRIAL 
JURISDICTION OVER CIVILIANS LIVING OR WORKING AT MILITARY POSTS ABROAD»

G MURDER CONVICTIONS AGAINST WIVES OF SERVICE PERSONNEL, ARE ALREADY 
SEFORE THE SUPREME COURT. : 

THE MILITARY APPEALS PANEL~-HIGHEST TRIBUNAL FOR COURT MARTIAL

NEW YORK, MARCH 31 CAP)=TREASURY AGENTS HAVE HAULED AWAY 
SUBSCRIPTION LISTS AND OTHER RECORDS FROM THE GOVERNMENT-SEIZED 
OFFICES OF THE COMMUNIST DAILY WORKER. 

REMOVAL OF THE RECORDS CAME AFTER THE GOVERNMENT TURNED DOWN AS 
PREMATURE A CASH BID FOR THE SEIZED PROPERTY. 

HARRY SACHER, ATTORNEY FOR THE NEWSPAPER, SAID YESTERDAY'S 
REMOVAL WAS BY AGREEMENT. HE SAID TREASURY OFFICIALS ASSURED HIM 
THE SUBSCRIPTION LISTS WOULD NOT BE TURNED OVER TO THE FBI. 

AN INTERNAL REVENUE SPOKESMAN IN WASHINGTON SAID THE SUBSCRIPTION 
CARDS CONTAIN DATA THAT "DEFINITELY RELATE TO INCOME*® IN THE 
GOVERNMENT'S TAX CASE. 

THE GOVERNMENT SEIZED OFFICES OF THE DAILY WORKER AND THE 
COMMUNIST PARTY TUESDAY ON INCOME TAX LIENS, CLAIMING THE PARTY OWES 
$389,265 IN BACK TAXES AND THE WORKER $46,409. 

YESTERDAY JOHN Js ABT, ATTORNEY FOR THE PARTY, AND SACHER 
OFFERED $2,850 FOR THE SEIZED PROPERTY. THEY PEGGED THE VALUE OF 
THE PROPERTY AT THAT AMOUNT. 

DONALD Re MOYSEY, NEW YORK DISTRICT DIRECTOR OF INTERNAL REVENUE, 
DECLINED THE OFFER FOR THE TIME BEING, SAYING AN INVENTORY OF THE 
PROPERTY WAS NOT COMPLETE. 

THE DAILY WORKER HAS CONTINUED PUBLICATION IN OTHER OFFICES IN THE 
SAME BUILDING WHERE ITS OWN PREMISES WERE PADLOCKED. 

MEANWHILE, DEFENSE ATTORNEYS YESTERDAY USED THE SEIZURE ACTION as 
THE BASIS FOR A MOTION TO POSTPONE THE TRIAL OF SEVEN SECOND-STRING 
COMMUNISTS SCHEDULED FOR APRIL 9. 

THE ATTORNEYS SAID THE SEIZURE HAD CREATED A "HOSTILE CLIMATE® 
THAT MADE IT IMPOSSIBLE FOR THE SEVEN TO GET A FAIR TRIAL ON 
CHARGES OF CONSPIRING TO TEACH AND ADVOCATE VIOLENT OVERTHROW OF 
THE GOVERNMENT. 

THE DEFENSE SUGGESTED THE CASE BE PUT OVER UNTIL THE FALL TERM, 

IN OPPOSING THE MOTION, CHIEF ASST. UeSe ATTY. THOMAS Be 
GILCHRIST JR. SAID THE DEFENSE WAS USING THE SEIZURE AS A 
CONVENIENT PRETEXT. 

FEDERAL JUDGE ALEXANDER BICKS RESERVED DECISIO¥. 

THE SEVEN DEFENDANTS ARE GEORGE Be CHARNEY, ALEXANDER 
TRA | S. ON BA JAMES Es JACKSON. \ ale 
SIDNEY STEIN AND FRED FINE. : » WILLIAM NORMAN,
